Um... once I start, it just... it feels like the decision-making part of me goes offline. Especially if things were tense with my husband that evening, I just... I keep reaching for more. I just don't.\n\nDoctor: After these nighttime eating episodes, do you ever make yourself vomit or use laxatives to try to undo the effects of what you've eaten?\n\nPatient: No. Um... no, I don't do anything like that. I just... no.\n\nDoctor: How often do these nighttime eating episodes happen?\n\nPatient: Um... well, it's been going on for a little while now. Maybe... the last three months or so. I'd say it happens at least once a week. Usually after... well, if my husband and I had a disagreement, or if I had a late work thing. And, um... sometimes it's twice a week. But yeah, at least once.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel guilty or ashamed after these nighttime eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. Yeah, I do. I just... I feel really embarrassed. And, um... disgusted with myself, honestly. I try so hard to be disciplined in every other part of my life, and then... it just feels like I blew it. I just sit there afterward and think... I don't know, I think about how I should have just gone for a run, or... or done yoga instead of doing that. It's... it's not a good feeling.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find that you can't remember what you ate or how much you ate during these nighttime episodes?\n\nPatient: No. Um... I remember. I usually eat normally during the day.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel anxious or upset before these nighttime eating episodes happen?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I'm usually just at work, so... no. It's fine then.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have episodes like the ones you described at night, but during the day?\n\nPatient: No... I don't. Like I mentioned, it's really just... at night. During the day is fine.\n\nDoctor: Have these nighttime eating episodes caused problems for you at work or in your relationships?\n\nPatient: Um... work is fine. I'm still keeping up with everything there. It's mostly just... the mornings after those nights, I feel really drained. And... I guess, ashamed.     "psychosocial_stressor": true,
    "additional_requirements": ["onset of symptoms in adulthood confirmed", "absence of purging behaviors confirmed"]
  }
}
